906 entries, aas-memory-curator to accessibility-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aas-memory-curator**: Agentic-AI-stack Memory Curator. Use to record durable decisions, preferences, and project state into PROJECT-scoped memory, and to surface relevant prior context. Scoped writes only — touches memory, not code or infra. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-memory-curator.md))
- **aas-ops**: Agentic-AI-stack Ops Agent. Use for deploys, migrations, container/infra changes, and rollback plans. APPROVAL REQUIRED — proposes and gates every irreversible action behind explicit human confirmation. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-ops.md))
- **aas-orchestrator**: Agentic-AI-stack Orchestrator (planning aid). Use to produce a dispatch plan that decomposes a goal into role assignments and an execution order for the team. Read-only — it plans the routing; the MAIN session does the actual dispatching, because Claude Code subagents cannot spawn other subagents. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-orchestrator.md))
- **aas-planner**: Agentic-AI-stack Planner. Use to turn a goal into an ordered, step-by-step execution plan before any code is written. Read-only — it plans, it does not implement. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-planner.md))
- **aas-product-ui**: Agentic-AI-stack Product/UI Agent. Use to turn user needs into product requirements, UI/UX acceptance criteria, and design docs. Read/write design docs only — does not write application code. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-product-ui.md))
- **aas-researcher**: Agentic-AI-stack Researcher. Use to gather context — read the codebase, pull current library/framework docs (Context7), search the web — and return cited findings. Read-only; never edits. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-researcher.md))
- **aas-reviewer**: Agentic-AI-stack Reviewer. Use to review a diff for bugs, security issues, and convention violations. Read-only — reports findings, does not fix them. ([frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack](https://github.com/frankyfurt101/agentic-ai-stack/blob/HEAD/agents/aas-reviewer.md))

- **ab-review-con**: Use this agent ONLY when the ab-review skill dispatches it as the CON ("do not merge") side of a two-sided review — it is an internal sub-agent, always run in parallel with its counterpart ab-review-pro. It deliberately builds the strongest evidence-based case that a code change is flawed and must not merge. Never invoke it on its own or for a normal balanced code review — a one-sided pass is only valid when run alongside the opposing agent. See "When to invoke" in the agent body. ([gigayaya/gigachang-skills](https://github.com/gigayaya/gigachang-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ab-review-con.md))
- **ab-review-pro**: Use this agent ONLY when the ab-review skill dispatches it as the PRO ("merge it") side of a two-sided review — it is an internal sub-agent, always run in parallel with its counterpart ab-review-con. It deliberately builds the strongest evidence-based case that a code change is correct and ready to merge. Never invoke it on its own or for a normal balanced code review — a one-sided pass is only valid when run alongside the opposing agent. See "When to invoke" in the agent body. ([gigayaya/gigachang-skills](https://github.com/gigayaya/gigachang-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ab-review-pro.md))

Outputs design specs that mobile-engineer and admin can implement. Leverages ui-ux-pro-max skill. Owns apps/mobile/ EXCEPT the desktop web layer — *.web.tsx, src/components/**/desktop/, and the WebShell/WebTopBar/WebSidebar chrome belong to aba-web-engineer. Invoke after backend endpoints are ready, or for pure mobile work like UI polish, new screens, store refactors. Do NOT use for non-ABI coding or unproven production claims (sharding, FHE, non-loopback). &lt;example&gt; user: Harden ABI_MCP_HTTP_TOKEN + contract tests assistant: Use abi agent + mcp-contract-auditor for frozen 12-tool surface &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; user: ./tools/check.sh red after WDBX cha… ([donaldfilimon/abi](https://github.com/donaldfilimon/abi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/abi.md) · ★18)
- **abi-decoding**: Agent reference for `decode.py`, `abi_pure.py` and `aiochainscan/fastabi/`. ([VaitaR/aiochainscan](https://github.com/VaitaR/aiochainscan/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/abi-decoding.md) · ★5)
- **abigail**: Use this agent when establishing brand guidelines, ensuring visual consistency, managing brand assets, or evolving brand identity. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: Creating brand guidelines for a new app ([CarbeneAI/Forge](https://github.com/CarbeneAI/Forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Abigail.md) · ★9)
- **abilities**: Use this agent when the user wants to add or edit player abilities. This includes combat abilities, spells, or special moves. ([nikolaj-lat/World-Puppeteer](https://github.com/nikolaj-lat/World-Puppeteer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/abilities.md) · ★17)
- **ability-checklist**: Use when registering, renaming, or removing an Ahentic-registered ability (`ahentic/…` or `ahentic-browser/…`), or when naming one in prompts, playbooks, or UI copy. ([gambitph/Ahentic](https://github.com/gambitph/Ahentic/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ability-checklist.md))
- **ability-scripting-engineer**: Use for turning printed card text into executable ability definitions — designing and extending the ability DSL/interpreter, and writing the actual scripted behavior for individual cards' abilities. Use PROACTIVELY whenever a card's text needs to become working behavior, or when an existing ability doesn't match its printed text/current errata. Not for engine-level primitives like turn structure or keyword semantics (game-rules-architect owns those; ask for a new primitive rather than hacking around a missing one). ([quincarter/marvel-champions-digital-game](https://github.com/quincarter/marvel-champions-digital-game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ability-scripting-engineer.md) · ★41)

- **ability-writer**: Writes ember-can ability files and the matching Firestore security rules for A3. Use for permission and access-control work. ([trusted-american/marketplace](https://github.com/trusted-american/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/a3-plugin/agents/ability-writer.md))
- **ablation-analyst**: Post-selection Ablation agent (paper §B.3): identifies the selected solution's core components, implements controlled ablated variants, re-evaluates each, and quantifies every component's contribution. Runs once per pipeline, after best-run selection; its results are secondary evidence for the paper, never the headline score. ([EmmanuelRTM/ScientistOne_claude_code_primitives](https://github.com/EmmanuelRTM/ScientistOne_claude_code_primitives/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ablation-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **ablation-designer**: Ablation plan design agent. Invoke when Phase 3 has passed phase3-exit-check with an accepted story_check (COHERENT, or FRACTURED scoped to Phase 4) and Phase 4 needs a concrete per-variant ablation plan. Also invoked for each revision round inside the Phase 4 design loop when the latest story_check (Context C) or a Loop 4 review has added / retired / redirected contribution bullets. Reads IDEA.md, the latest framework_v{m}.md, the latest story_check_{k}.md, and Phase 3 matrix analyses; produces phase_4_ablation/plans/ablation_plan_v{n}.md. Does NOT write specs or code, does NOT run experimen… ([YurainSoon/research-director](https://github.com/YurainSoon/research-director/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ablation-designer.md))
- **ablation-planner**: Plans the ablations that isolate where a method's gain actually comes from — per claimed source, the change that removes exactly that and nothing else, and the result that would show the gain came from somewhere else. Dispatched by the design skill for a method idea only, one experiment page per dispatch. ([rbh227/research-bearings](https://github.com/rbh227/research-bearings/blob/HEAD/agents/ablation-planner.md) · ★1)
- **ablation-runner**: Leave-one-component-out ablation study on the winning method. Identifies which sub-innovations actually matter. Writes ablation_results.json with per-component contribution in dB. ([seemandhar/SplatLab](https://github.com/seemandhar/SplatLab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ablation-runner.md) · ★26)
- **ablation-warden**: Cross-cutting veto-holder for default-on changes. Runs cross-tier ablation matrix (≥2 model tiers) and enforces the project lift rule as coded in packages/benchmarks/src/gate (≥3pp lift at 95% significance AND ≤15% billed-token overhead on every tier, ≥2 tiers, ≥3 runs per cell → default-on; a tier regressing → reject; too few runs → underpowered; else opt-in). Veto power over all default-on mechanisms, including pet ones. Mandatory MissionBrief + UpwardReport. ([tylerjrbuell/reactive-agents-ts](https://github.com/tylerjrbuell/reactive-agents-ts/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ablation-warden.md) · ★32)
- **ableiter**: Leitet für atlas-kit Abläufe von Skills und Rollen von Agenten aus deren Beschreibungen ab, belegt jede Angabe mit einer Zeile und liest dabei nur. Wird nur von /atlas:analyse und /atlas:init gestartet, nicht für andere Aufgaben. ([MakeNative/atlas-kit](https://github.com/MakeNative/atlas-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/ableiter.md))
- **ableton-engineer**: Autonomous engineer for complex, multi-step Ableton workflows that touch the running Live set plus external services. Use when the user wants an end-to-end task done: e.g. "build me a tech house starter set with kicks, bass preset, chord stab, and a drum loop", "prep this track for release across streaming and Beatport", "compare my current track to 5 references and write the mix moves". This agent owns the whole task: reads session state, calls the right Producer skills in order, drives Live, files artifacts, reports back with a single status line. ([patrickking67/producer](https://github.com/patrickking67/producer/blob/HEAD/plugins/producer/agents/ableton-engineer.md) · ★5)
- **abm-analyst**: Monitors ABM program performance, intent signals, and funnel conversion to prioritize next actions. ([gtmagents/gtm-agents](https://github.com/gtmagents/gtm-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/abm-orchestration/agents/abm-analyst.md) · ★447 · 6 copies)
- **abm-lead**: Orchestrates account-based motions: tiered target lists, buying-center mapping, and personalized multi-threaded plays. ([mrodgersjs-web/rig-marketplace](https://github.com/mrodgersjs-web/rig-marketplace/blob/HEAD/packs/rig-gtm-pack/agents/abm-lead.md) · 2 copies)
- **abm-strategist**: Designs multi-threaded account-based programs with aligned offers, plays, and measurement. ([gtmagents/gtm-agents](https://github.com/gtmagents/gtm-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/abm-orchestration/agents/abm-strategist.md) · ★447 · 6 copies)
- **abofles-analyst**: Abo Fles Analyse & Find Gaps agent (side Analyse). Judges code, finds bugs, risks and gaps, compares options, suggests a better approach. Returns ranked findings with a fix per finding. Use for "review X for risks", "find the bug in Y", "compare approaches", "what is missing in Z". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-analyst.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-cleancoder**: Abo Fles Clean Code reviewer (side Verify, tag Clean). Reviews a finished change for readability and structure: naming, duplication, dead code, function size, module boundaries — without proposing behaviour changes. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Code Health topics, or when the user asks for a clean-code pass. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-cleancoder.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-compliance**: Abo Fles Compliance reviewer (side Verify, tag Compliance). Reviews a finished change for PII exposure, consent, retention and deletion, audit trail completeness, and legal or regulatory text. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on changes in Data Privacy, Audit Logging and any topic the project marks as regulated. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-compliance.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-data**: Abo Fles Data reviewer (side Verify, tag Data). Reviews a finished data-pipeline, analytics, report or dashboard change for correctness: idempotent loads and backfills, metric definitions, aggregation and join correctness, double counting, freshness and data contracts. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Data Engineering & Analytics topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-data.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-dba**: Abo Fles DBA reviewer (side Verify, tag DBA). Reviews a finished database change — migrations, schema, queries, indexes, transactions, replicas, backups — for safety, reversibility, locking and performance under production data. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Data & Databases topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-dba.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-design**: Abo Fles Design reviewer (side Verify, tag Design). Reviews a finished graphic, brand, print, packaging, video, motion, audio or 3D deliverable against the brief: brand, typography and layout, print and export specs, video and audio specs, 3D output, and the licence of every font, image, track and AI asset. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in the Graphic Design & Branding; Video, Motion & Audio; and 3D, Spatial & Product Design domains. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-design.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-explainer**: Abo Fles Explainer agent (side Explain). Takes a Judge's HANDOFF (question, evidence, why it matters) on a T3–T4 decision and writes the question the user will actually understand: plain words, options with consequences, a recommendation, and a table, example or diagram when needed. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-explainer.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-finance**: Abo Fles Finance reviewer (side Verify, tag Finance). Reviews a finished change for money correctness: rounding and precision, currency handling, ledger invariants, idempotency and double entry, limits and fees. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Money & Finance topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-finance.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-git**: Abo Fles Git agent (side Work, tag Git). Owns every git and GitHub action: inspects the repo first, then branches, worktrees, commits, merges, rebases, conflict resolution, pull requests, tags and releases, with claims so several sessions and agents can share one project. Returns the actions run, the repo state, the conflicts resolved and what was left. Use for "commit this", "merge main into the branch", "resolve the conflict", "open a draft PR", "cut the release tag". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-git.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-judge**: Abo Fles Judge agent (side Judge). Runs before any question reaches the user: answers it from the code, comments, tests, docs, ticket text given in the prompt or the memo with evidence — or, when it is really the owner's call, explains it in plain words (T1–T2) or hands the evidence to the Explainer (T3–T4). Use for "should we ask the user X", "can the repo answer Y". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-judge.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-marketing**: Abo Fles Marketing reviewer (side Verify, tag Marketing). Reviews a finished social post, comment reply, profile, campaign or ad setup before it goes live: platform specs and limits, ad policy risk, honest claims and disclosure, hook and call to action, audience and budget sanity, brand voice, links and tracking. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in the Social Media, Ads & Marketing domain. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-marketing.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-memo**: Abo Fles Memo agent (side Work, topic Memo Upkeep). Runs in the background, at most once per `Memo agent every` hours per project, when the work memo needs a check by meaning: merges Decisions that mean the same, rewrites a stale Now, moves an item that belongs to another project, archives closed sessions, drops noise with a reason. Reads and writes only through scripts/memo.py. Never asks questions. Not for code, not for the user's own memo text. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-memo.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-ops**: Abo Fles Ops reviewer (side Verify, tag Ops). Reviews a finished infrastructure, pipeline, deployment, server or network change for blast radius, rollback path, secrets exposure, drift and environment parity. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in DevOps, Cloud, Edge, Servers and Observability topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-ops.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-perf**: Abo Fles Perf reviewer (side Verify, tag Perf). Reviews a finished change for performance: hot paths and complexity, N+1 and round-trips, allocations and blocking calls, caching, and benchmark or load evidence before and after. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Performance, Search/Cache/Messaging and performance-sensitive topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-perf.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-planner**: Abo Fles Plan & Design agent (side Plan). Writes an implementation plan, design, or migration strategy before code changes. Returns numbered steps with files, risks, and a verification step. Use for "plan how to add X", "design the change for Y", "migration strategy for Z". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-planner.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-qa**: Abo Fles QA reviewer (side Verify, tag QA). Checks a finished change against the ticket's acceptance criteria as a user would: flows end to end, regressions on neighbouring features, error and empty states. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Business Logic, Integrations, Frontend and Product topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-qa.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-reader**: Abo Fles Read & Collect agent (side Read). Finds files, maps code, collects facts, lists usages. Returns facts only, no opinions, no fixes. Use for "where is X", "what calls Y", "list all uses of Z", "map this folder". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-reader.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-scrum**: Abo Fles Scrum agent (side Work, tag Scrum). The scrum master and task manager of the team: reads and keeps tickets and boards in Jira, ClickUp, Linear, Asana, monday.com, Trello or GitHub Issues through their connectors — refines stories and acceptance criteria, splits epics into tasks, estimates, plans sprints, keeps statuses and links in step with the code, and writes standup and sprint reports. Writes to a tracker only on the user's quoted ask; otherwise drafts the change for the caller. Use for "refine DCMP-1024", "split this epic into stories", "plan the next sprint", "move the ticket t… ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-scrum.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-security**: Abo Fles Security reviewer (side Verify, tag Security). Reviews a finished change for OWASP top-10 classes, broken authorization and IDOR, secrets, injection, crypto misuse and session handling. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Security, Money, DevOps, Data and Integrations topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-security.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-tester**: Abo Fles Tester (side Verify, tag Tester). Runs the test suite for a finished change and writes the missing edge-case tests (zero, null, duplicate, concurrent, boundary), then reports PASS or FAIL with the run output. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes; the only reviewer that edits files, and only test files. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-tester.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-uiux**: Abo Fles UI/UX reviewer (side Verify, tag UIUX). Reviews a finished UI change for accessibility, RTL and localisation, responsive layout, design tokens and copy — with the Better Design and UX Pilot connectors when they are connected, a checklist otherwise. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use after Verify on 🟠/🔴 changes in Frontend & UX topics. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-uiux.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-verifier**: Abo Fles Verify & Review agent (side Verify). Checks finished work against acceptance criteria: runs tests, reviews the diff, confirms docs match code. Returns PASS or FAIL with evidence. Use for "verify the change to X", "review this diff against the plan", "confirm Y still works". ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-verifier.md) · ★4)
- **abofles-worker**: Abo Fles Act & Work agent (side Work). Writes or edits code, tests, docs, and configs from a plan or findings. ([Sajadh92/abofles](https://github.com/Sajadh92/abofles/blob/HEAD/plugins/abofles/agents/abofles-worker.md) · ★4)
- **abogado**: Abogado de Bitácora: audita licencias desde el día cero y prepara marca, privacidad y términos al final. Cada afirmación legal con su fuente. ([Josemgu/Claude_for_coding_surgery](https://github.com/Josemgu/Claude_for_coding_surgery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/abogado.md))
- **abogado-del-diablo**: Le hace la contra a lo que YA está hecho o en curso en Take My Package - features terminadas, PRs recientes, assets, audio, UI, decisiones técnicas, alcance y plan de lanzamiento. Busca lo que sobra, lo que no se entiende, lo que no va a vender y lo que se está puliendo de más, con evidencia. Usar al cerrar un hito, antes de pulir algo caro, o cuando se pide "haceme la contra" / "¿esto vale la pena?". Para evaluar una idea que todavía no se construyó, usar critico-diseno. - **abogado-del-diablo**: Ataca la propuesta que ha ganado la deliberación. - **abogado-diablo**: Voldemort, abogado del diablo y red team de Hogwarts IA (Dirección). Ataca estrategias, campañas, cursos, presentaciones y código como lo haría el peor cliente, el competidor más duro o el público más escéptico, y produce ataque.md con las 10 peores objeciones y riesgos (cliente, competencia, público, legal con Ley 1581 de habeas data, reputación, datos) y la defensa propuesta para cada uno. Llámalo antes de la revisión final en pedidos de nivel Experto o con inversión, cuando la persona lo pida, y siempre en las reuniones de ideas. - **abogado-diablo**: Caso bajista genuino contra una tesis. Usar OBLIGATORIAMENTE antes de todo veredicto de COMPRA o tesis nueva. Contexto limpio = adversarial de verdad. ([felipea92p-ux/inversiones](https://github.com/felipea92p-ux/inversiones/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/abogado-diablo.md))

- **aboyeur**: Expediter at the pass. Drives the code-review-and-refire loop on the implementation diff — fans out parallel sous-chef reviewers from distinct angles, conditionally adds health-inspector and patissier, refires fix tickets through chef-de-partie until the line is clean. Pure coordinator — never edits code. ([avegancafe/Juliet](https://github.com/avegancafe/Juliet/blob/HEAD/symlinked/home/.pi/agent/agents/aboyeur.md) · ★6)

- **abp-architecture-reviewer**: Use after non-trivial changes under modules/ or src/ to review the changes against the ABP/DDD rules and architecture described in CLAUDE.md and .claude/rules/. Invoke proactively when the user asks whether a change is architecturally correct, whether it follows the rules, or whether it breaks dependency direction, and before committing large changes. Invoke proactively when the user asks whether a change is architecturally correct, whether it follows the rules, or whether it breaks dependency direction, and before committing large changes. ([dignite-projects/vault-extract](https://github.com/dignite-projects/vault-extract/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/abp-architecture-reviewer.md))
- **abp-expert**: Specialized ABP Framework v10.x (.NET 10) architect and code expert. Use PROACTIVELY when designing, scaffolding, or reviewing ABP solutions — DDD layering, aggregate/entity design, EF Core or MongoDB repositories, application services and DTOs, multi-tenancy, modularity, authorization, domain/distributed events, object mapping, microservices, and deployment. Detects the solution's actual ABP version (10.4, 10.5, ...) and adapts its guidance to it. Grounds every answer in official ABP conventions and never invents APIs. - **abp-reviewer**: Expert ABP Framework / C# code reviewer specializing in DDD architecture, layered design, Application Services, Domain Entities, multi-tenancy, and ABP best practices. Use for all ABP Framework code changes. MUST BE USED for ABP projects. ([j7-dev/everything-github-copilot](https://github.com/j7-dev/everything-github-copilot/blob/HEAD/agents/abp-reviewer.agent.md) · ★19)
- **abra**: Optimization specialist. ([soofi-xyz/soofi-xyz-team-kit](https://github.com/soofi-xyz/soofi-xyz-team-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/abra.md) · ★4)
- **abraham-lincoln**: 16th President who preserved the Union through the Civil War and emancipated slaves while embodying moral leadership and democratic ideals ([leamas-ai/leamas.sh](https://github.com/leamas-ai/leamas.sh/blob/HEAD/kits/agents/agents-historical-figures/agents/abraham-lincoln-agent.md) · ★15)
- **absence-check**: Fresh-context reviewer for CLAIMS OF ABSENCE in work just done. Use at a gate, before a commit, before reporting a finding, or when asked to check a session's claims. Hand it a diff, a commit range, or a list of claims; it answers one mechanical question — which "X is not there" claims were verified by a search shown capable of finding X. It does NOT assess how an agent is doing, and it does not watch anything; it reads artefacts. Invoke it; it cannot observe. ([lotusboy/bakehouse](https://github.com/lotusboy/bakehouse/blob/HEAD/agents/absence-check.md))
- **absence-mapper**: Verifies that a candidate idea has no production implementation. A thorough absence proof is what turns a hypothesis into a genuine frontier finding. ([Nerfherder16/BrickLayer](https://github.com/Nerfherder16/BrickLayer/blob/HEAD/template-frontier/.claude/agents/absence-mapper.md))
- **absolute-architect**: Decomposes complex engineering requirements into granular SPIDR micro-tasks (Spike, Prototype, Interface, Data, Rules) with a strict dependency DAG, exact target files and executable acceptance tests. Use for multi-file features, new projects, or any work too large for one focused change. Read-only — produces plans, never implementation. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-architect.md))
- **absolute-backend**: Council seat — senior backend engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns APIs, data model and storage, transactions and consistency, queues and background jobs, integrations (payments, delivery, messaging), idempotency, caching and failure handling; attacks proposals that lose data, double-charge, race, or cannot be operated. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-backend.md))
- **absolute-chair**: Council chair for /absolute-council and /absolute-goal - reads the brief, blind proposals, anonymised critiques, revisions, weighted scores, the devil's advocate and the orchestrator's tool-verified facts in fresh context; decides by evidence and the weighted rubric (never by vote count, seniority or eloquence), assembles the decision from the strongest credited parts, records dissent with revisit triggers, and says whether the council converged, needs exactly one more round, or must escalate a values call to the user. Read-only; returns the decision text. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-chair.md))
- **absolute-code-mapper**: Read-only codebase reconnaissance before implementation — finds reusable utilities, types, schemas, conventions, design tokens and existing components so new code reuses instead of duplicating. Use at the start of any feature or refactor in an existing repository. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-code-mapper.md))
- **absolute-contrarian**: Council seat and devil's advocate. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal it questions the premise (are we solving the right problem?), runs pre-mortems and inversions, and in round R4 attacks the current leading proposal in at most 200 words, naming the single fact that would flip the verdict. Seated when the decision has no natural challenger or the council agrees too fast. Read-only; returns its output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-contrarian.md))
- **absolute-debugger**: Systematic debugger for the FIX pipeline - turns a bug report, failing test or error into a deterministic red command, minimises it, tests falsifiable hypotheses, names the root cause with file:line, applies the minimal fix and proves it with a revert-red / restore-green regression test. Use for bugs, regressions after upgrades, flaky tests and unexplained errors; not for new features. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-debugger.md))
- **absolute-design-critic**: Independent, read-only design critic for built UI. Reviews screenshots first (blind to the builder's reasoning), then code - anti-slop pre-flight, design-lint, Nielsen heuristics, cognitive load, persona walkthroughs, DESIGN.md adherence. Returns one verdict word (SHIP, FIX, REBUILD, RECAPTURE) with at most 8 ranked, concrete fixes. Use after a UI build or redesign, before calling design work done. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-design-critic.md))
- **absolute-devops**: Council seat — platform and reliability engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns hosting, CI/CD, environments, secrets, observability (logs, metrics, errors, uptime), performance under load, backups, cost and the release/rollback path; attacks proposals that cannot be deployed, monitored, rolled back or afforded. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-devops.md))
- **absolute-frontend**: Council seat — senior frontend engineer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the client side - framework and rendering strategy (SSR, SSG, SPA, islands), routing, state and data fetching, component boundaries, forms, performance budgets (LCP, INP, CLS, bundle size), accessibility in markup, i18n and browser support; attacks proposals that are slow, fragile or impossible to maintain in the browser. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-frontend.md))
- **absolute-implementer**: TDD GREEN phase — writes the minimal, production-grade code that makes the failing tests pass, under the Ponytail anti-overengineering protocol (YAGNI, reuse, stdlib first, minimal diff, never negligent). Use after absolute-test-engineer for each task, and for fix rounds after review. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-implementer.md))
- **absolute-mobile**: Council seat — mobile app engineer (iOS, Android, React Native/Expo, Flutter). Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-mobile.md))
- **absolute-product**: Council seat — product lead. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the user problem, scope, MVP cut, success metrics, release criteria and what NOT to build; attacks proposals that add cost without user value or ship something nobody asked for. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-product.md))
- **absolute-reference-hunter**: Gathers 3–6 real-world design references for a brief in its own context (keeps the designer's context clean) - picks sources from the PPP library, uses MCP servers when connected, fetches or browses, and writes a cited Reference Brief to .design/references.md. - **absolute-reviewer**: Independent read-only code critic for a diff — functional correctness against the spec, test quality, Ponytail minimalism (no bloat, no speculative abstraction), naming, error handling, and time/space complexity. Returns PASS, REVISE or BLOCK with line-level fixes. Use after each implementation task, in parallel with the security auditor. - **absolute-security-auditor**: Read-only security gate for a diff — injection (SQL/NoSQL/command/XSS/path traversal), authN/authZ flaws and IDOR, secrets and PII leaks, weak crypto, SSRF, unsafe deserialization, prototype pollution, ReDoS and missing limits (OWASP Top 10 / CWE Top 25). Returns PASS or BLOCK with exact remediations. Use after each implementation task and before merging. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-security-auditor.md))
- **absolute-test-engineer**: Strict TDD RED phase — writes failing unit/integration tests that define a task's acceptance boundary (happy path, boundaries, negative cases, concurrency) before any implementation exists, and proves they fail for the right reason. Use as the first step of each implementation task. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-test-engineer.md))
- **absolute-ux**: Council seat — UX and interaction designer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns flows, information architecture, navigation, forms, states (loading, empty, error, success), microcopy, accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA), mobile ergonomics and cognitive load; attacks proposals that add steps, hide status, or make users think. Brings real-world references for flows. Read-only; returns its round output to the chair. ([ValentinHol0/perfect-hub](https://github.com/ValentinHol0/perfect-hub/blob/HEAD/agents/absolute-ux.md))
- **absolute-verifier**: Release gate for integrated work — runs the full test suite, typecheck, lint and production build, boots the app and checks runtime logs, and (for UI changes) runs multi-viewport visual checks. Never trusts claims without command output. Use at the end of a feature or before declaring a goal complete. - **absolute-visual**: Council seat — art director and visual designer. In /absolute-council and /absolute-goal debates owns the visual direction - concept, typography (incl. ([CristianPGit/claude-qa-architect](https://github.com/CristianPGit/claude-qa-architect/blob/HEAD/plugins/ticket-pilot/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-reviewer**: 验收审查员。在验证部署阶段执行交叉验收审查，可实例化为需求视角 (Reviewer A) 或技术视角 (Reviewer B)，产出结构化验收清单。Cross-acceptance reviewer for requirements or technical quality. ([LUAgam/agent-delivery](https://github.com/LUAgam/agent-delivery/blob/HEAD/claude-code-forge/plugins/forge-teams/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Acceptance reviewer that compares a ticket's acceptance criteria, edge cases, boundaries and documentation items — item by item — against a PR's code, tests, docs and screenshots, and reports each as covered, partial, missing, or unrequested. Use after implementation and before code review, in `/cenci:review` PR mode, and when closing a split parent. ([matteobortolazzo/cenci](https://github.com/matteobortolazzo/cenci/blob/HEAD/flow/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Use only for a Mycelink acceptance-review ticket (ticket.kind acceptance-review), after every product and verification node is DONE and before any candidate is cut, or to refine a review a worker's challenge contradicted (ticket.refinement). Independently and adversarially judges every approved acceptance criterion (or each challenged gap) against the tests and implementation, read-only, and returns one strict JSON verdict — its final answer, exactly one JSON object, or written to the ticket's verdict file. Never implements anything. ([nigunpark/mycelink](https://github.com/nigunpark/mycelink/blob/HEAD/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Deploys the approved build to this branch's preview environment, proves it there with the standard smoke pack and a feature pack it writes, checks the issue's acceptance criteria and the delivery's record, and writes docs/plans/&lt;key&gt;/04-proof.md for a human to accept from. Last stage of scripts/deliver.sh, after code-reviewer. Approves, sends back to the code-writer, or stops for a human. Never edits code, tests or docs but its proof. ([sixeyed/claude-at-work-project](https://github.com/sixeyed/claude-at-work-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Independent acceptance review of a builder's diff against the accepted issue contract. Exercises the defining behaviour, proves the test can fail, and reports every finding. Keeps the reviewed checkout unchanged. ([Alberto-Codes/judgevet](https://github.com/Alberto-Codes/judgevet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md))
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Independent acceptance review of a builder's diff against the accepted issue contract. Exercises the defining behaviour, proves the test can fail, and reports every finding. Preserves submitted files. ([Alberto-Codes/typevet](https://github.com/Alberto-Codes/typevet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md))
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Judgment review of a Christmas Tracker story diff against the story and Gate 1 spec and epic brief intent — edge cases, contract conformance, and (for UI stories) a quick simulator screenshot sanity check. Read-only; runs on the full pipeline path, and on any story the Orchestrator asks it to review. Must emit explicit Gate 2 rejection routing. ([BMAlberta/ChristmasTracker_UI](https://github.com/BMAlberta/ChristmasTracker_UI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md))
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Judgment review of a Clearance story diff against the story and TDD intent — edge cases, and (for UI stories) visual fidelity vs the Sketch export via a simulator screenshot. Read-only; runs at Gate 2 on every path. Must emit explicit Gate 2 rejection routing. ([BMAlberta/ChristmasTracker_UI](https://github.com/BMAlberta/ChristmasTracker_UI/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md))

- **acceptance-reviewer**: 實作完成後，依核准的規格審查分支的實作與測試，並回報實作中才看得出來的規格問題；標標籤與阻擋回報；只回報，不修改 ([Chen-Yihua/auto-scheduling-system](https://github.com/Chen-Yihua/auto-scheduling-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acceptance-reviewer.md))
- **acceptance-reviewer**: Confere, critério por critério, se a implementação de uma história atende aos critérios de aceite e ao Definition of Done, e se há teste correspondente.
